# Household hh_s15 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple_plus_one. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, entry, kitchen, living, office.

Residents:

- **Omar** (resident_1): works outside the home; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.55); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.38); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.30); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: baking, journaling, plants. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Felix** (resident_2): works from home; untidy (tidiness 0.50); average timing (jitter scale 1.20); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.78); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the office desk. Hobbies: board_games, napping, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Yuki** (resident_3): retired; untidy (tidiness 0.44); punctual (jitter scale 0.68); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.62); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the bedroom_2 desk. Hobbies: baking, friends, gaming. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, vacuuming.

## Day 3 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d3`: Felix hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:44–16:43).
- `ep:early_night:resident_1:d3`: Omar turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_1:d3`: Omar is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:05–16:30).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d3`: Omar has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `distracted:resident_2`: Felix is distracted (0.81): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `running_late:resident_2`: Felix is running late all day (0.83): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

## Day 12 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:deadline:resident_2:d11:due_today`: Felix's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_2:d11:relief`: Felix made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d12`: Omar gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:24–21:09).
- `ep:rough_day_at_work:resident_1:d12`: Omar had a rough day at work: goes quiet in the evening, keeps the phone close, the jacket lands on the couch (17:30–24:00).
- `distracted:resident_1`: Omar is distracted (0.83): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Felix has low energy (0.11): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `running_late:resident_2`: Felix is running late all day (0.84): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Omar energy 0.76, hurriedness 0.33, distraction 0.83; Felix energy 0.11, hurriedness 0.84, distraction 0.33; Yuki energy 0.78, hurriedness 0.33, distraction 0.72

- `low_energy:resident_2`: Felix has low energy (0.19): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 16 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `sick_day:resident_1` (Omar): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Omar has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `running_late:resident_2`: Felix is running late all day (0.74): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Omar energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.00, distraction 0.47; Felix energy 0.42, hurriedness 0.74, distraction 0.57; Yuki energy 0.65, hurriedness 0.20, distraction 0.52

## Day 19 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d19`: Omar has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Omar): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Omar has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 20 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:early_night:resident_1:d20`: Omar turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_3:d20`: Yuki gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:38–21:23).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_2:d20`: Felix is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (14:47–18:19).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d20`: Omar has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Omar): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Omar has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `running_late:resident_2`: Felix is running late all day (0.78): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Omar energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.00, distraction 0.31; Felix energy 0.34, hurriedness 0.78, distraction 0.29; Yuki energy 0.86, hurriedness 0.22, distraction 0.43

## Day 23 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d23`: Omar is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:43–15:13).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_1:d23`: Omar is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:47–17:12).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Omar): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Omar has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Felix has low energy (0.23): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Omar energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.00, distraction 0.56; Felix energy 0.23, hurriedness 0.59, distraction 0.45; Yuki energy 0.82, hurriedness 0.53, distraction 0.50

## Day 25 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d25`: Omar hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:40–16:36).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d25`: Omar is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:42–16:12).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_2:d25`: Felix is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (14:11–17:32).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_2:d25`: Felix is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (10:27–12:27).
- `distracted:resident_3`: Yuki is distracted (0.89): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `running_late:resident_2`: Felix is running late all day (0.74): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d27`: Felix is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:01–16:31).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_3:d27`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:28–16:58).

- `distracted:resident_3`: Yuki is distracted (0.83): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.

## Day 29 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d29`: Felix is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:33–16:03).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_3:d29`: Yuki can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:05–24:00).
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Omar has low energy (0.18): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Omar energy 0.18, hurriedness 0.25, distraction 0.38; Felix energy 0.36, hurriedness 0.46, distraction 0.39; Yuki energy 0.69, hurriedness 0.13, distraction 0.55
